266: function localTime(value) {
267:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
268:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
269:     return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
270:   if (typeof value === "number") {
271:     const totalMinutes = Math.round((value % 1) * 24 * 60);
272:     return `${String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
273:   }
274:   const text = String(value).trim();
275:   if (/^0\.\d+$/.test(text)) return localTime(Number(text));
276:   const match = text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
277:   return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}` : text;
278: }

280: function classNumberSafe(value) { return String(value || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""); }

266: function localTime(value) {
267:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
268:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
269:     return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
270:   if (typeof value === "number") {
271:     const totalMinutes = Math.round((value % 1) * 24 * 60);
272:     return `${String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
273:   }
274:   const text = String(value).trim();
275:   if (/^0\.\d+$/.test(text)) return localTime(Number(text));
276:   const match = text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
277:   return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}` : text;
278: }

266: function localTime(value) {
267:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
268:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
269:     return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
270:   if (typeof value === "number") {
271:     const totalMinutes = Math.round((value % 1) * 24 * 60);
272:     return `${String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
273:   }
274:   const text = String(value).trim();
275:   if (/^0\.\d+$/.test(text)) return localTime(Number(text));
276:   const match = text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
277:   return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}` : text;
278: }

247: function localDate(value) {
248:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
249:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
250:     return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
251:   const serialDate = serialToDate(value);
252:   if (serialDate) return serialDate;
253:   const text = String(value).trim();
254:   const match = text.match(/(\d{4})[.\-/년]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\-/월]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
255:   if (match) return `${match[1]}-${match[2].padStart(2, "0")}-${match[3].padStart(2, "0")}`;
256:   const parsedText = new Date(text);
257:   if (!Number.isNaN(parsedText.getTime()) && /GMT|UTC|^[A-Za-z]{3}/.test(text))
258:     return `${parsedText.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(parsedText.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(parsedText.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
259:   const compact=text.replace(/\D/g,'');

262:   if(monthDay)return '2026-'+monthDay[1].padStart(2,'0')+'-'+monthDay[2].padStart(2,'0');
263:   return text;
264: }

1582:   const sheetSerialToDateTime=(value)=>{
1583:     if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
1584:     const raw=String(value).trim();
1585:     if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
1586:       const serial=Number(raw);
1587:       if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
1588:         const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
1589:         if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
1590:           const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),m=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
1591:           const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
1592:           return `${y}-${m}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
1593:         }
1594:       }
1595:     }

887: async function sensitiveHash(value){
888:   const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(`UEP:SENSITIVE:${String(value||'')}`);
889:   const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);
890:   return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
891: }

945: async function setSensitivePasswordFlow(){
946:   if(!canRevealStudentProtection())return toast('관리자·학년부장 권한에서 설정할 수 있습니다.');
947:   const configured=sensitivePasswordConfigured();
948:   const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});
949:   if(!result)return;
950:   if(configured&&await sensitiveHash(result.current)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
951:     return toast('현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
952:   }
953:   const passwordHash=await sensitiveHash(result.first);
954:   localStorage.setItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY,JSON.stringify({passwordHash,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));
955:   lockSensitiveSession();
956:   toast('민감정보 열람 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');
957:   if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');
958: }
959: async function unlockSensitiveInfo(){
960:   if(sensitiveSessionOpen())return true;
961:   if(!sensitivePasswordConfigured()){
962:     toast('설정 → 사용자·보안에서 먼저 비밀번호를 설정해 주세요.');
963:     return false;
964:   }
965:   const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'unlock',configured:true});
966:   if(!result)return false;
967:   if(await sensitiveHash(result.first)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
968:     toast('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
969:     return false;
970:   }
971:   uepSensitiveUnlockedUntil=Date.now()+10*60*1000;
972:   return true;
973: }
974: window.addEventListener('beforeunload',lockSensitiveSession);

1060: function studentFocusStudent(){
1061:   if(!studentFocusContext)return null;
1062:   return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(student=>String(student.id)===String(studentFocusContext.studentId)||String(student.studentNo)===String(studentFocusContext.studentNo))||null;

1064: function startStudentFocus(student, origin={}){
1065:   if(!student)return;

6776: function uniqueActiveAlertStudents(rows=[]){
6777:   const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
6778:   const validIds=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.id||s.studentId||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
6779:   const validNos=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.studentNo||s.number||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
6780:   const seen=new Set(),out=[];
6781:   for(const row of rows){
6782:     const id=String(row?.studentId||row?.id||'').trim(),no=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||'').trim();
6783:     const hasKnownIdentity=(id&&validIds.has(id))||(no&&validNos.has(no));
6784:     if((validIds.size||validNos.size)&&!hasKnownIdentity)continue;
6785:     const key=(id&&validIds.has(id)?id:'')||(no&&validNos.has(no)?no:'')||`${row?.classNo||row?.classLabel||''}|${row?.name||''}`;
6786:     if(!key||seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);out.push(row);
6787:   }
6788:   return out;
6789: }

6828:       return `<article class="work-board-row ${item.status==='종료'?'done':''}"><button class="work-board-main" data-work-notice-detail="${id}" data-work-notice-source="connected"><span class="work-board-state">직접</span><div><b>${title}</b><div class="work-notice-badges">${badges}</div><small>${escapeHtml(item.department||item.author||"1학년부")} · ${escapeHtml(item.postDate||item.date||"")}${item.deadline?` · 마감 ${escapeHtml(item.deadline)}`:""}</small>${progress}${item.detail?`<p>${escapeHtml(item.detail)}</p>`:""}</div><em>열기 ›</em></button>${currentUserCanManageNotices()?`<div class="work-board-row-actions"><button data-notice-edit="${id}">수정</button><button class="danger" data-notice-delete="${id}">삭제</button></div>`:""}</article>`;

9472: function studentAccessContext(student){
9473:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
9474:   const studentClass=String(student?.className||student?.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
9475:   const ownClass=String(scope.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
9476:   const role=currentRoleId();
9477:   const isAdmin=role==="admin";
9478:   const isOwnHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&ownClass&&studentClass&&String(Number(ownClass))===String(Number(studentClass));
9479:   const isOtherHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&!isOwnHomeroom;
9480:   const isReadOnlyLeadership=role==="vice_principal"||role==="principal";
9481:   return {role,isAdmin,isOwnHomeroom,isOtherHomeroom,isReadOnlyLeadership,canEditContact:!isReadOnlyLeadership&&(isAdmin||isOwnHomeroom)};
9482: }
9483: function sensitiveRevealKey(student,card){return `${student?.id||student?.studentNo||"unknown"}:${card}`;}
9484: function isStudentSensitiveCardMasked(student,card){
9485:   const access=studentAccessContext(student);
9486:   // 성적·입시는 상담/진학 협업을 위해 타반도 조회하되 UEP에서는 수정하지 않는다.
9487:   if(card==="score") return false;
9488:   // 연락처·이메일·보호자 연락처는 타반 담임에게 기본 마스킹하고 필요 시 현재 화면에서만 일시 공개한다.
9489:   return card==="contact"&&(access.isOtherHomeroom||access.isReadOnlyLeadership)&&!studentSensitiveRevealKeys.has(sensitiveRevealKey(student,card));
9490: }
9491: function maskedStudentCardMarkup(card,label){
9492:   return `<button type="button" class="student-sensitive-mask" data-reveal-student-card="${escapeHtml(card)}"><b>＊＊＊＊</b><span>${escapeHtml(label)} · 클릭하여 보기</span><small>보호 정보는 필요할 때만 현재 화면에서 일시적으로 공개됩니다.</small></button>`;
9493: }
9494: function studentWithContactOverride(student){return {...student};}

9497:   if(!access.canEditContact)return toast('이 학생의 연락정보 수정 권한이 없습니다.');
